Bank 3Bank 4Bank 6
; Creature sprites for each dungeon level (127,358,469)
8000: BB 9D ;Level 0(Never used); table read by 80B0,80B6
8002: BB 9D ;Level 1
8004: BB 9D ;Level 2
8006: 7B 98 ;Level 3
8008: 9B 9A ;Level 4
800A: 7B 98 ;Level 5
800C: 9B 9A ;Level 6
800E: BB 9D ;Level 7
8010: 7B 98 ;Level 8
8012: 9B 9A ;Level 9

; Boss sprites for each dungeon level, (1257,3468,9)
8014: DB 9F ;Level 0(Never used); table read by 80C0,80C6
8016: DB 9F ;Level 1
8018: DB 9F ;Level 2
801A: DB A3 ;Level 3
801C: DB A3 ;Level 4
801E: DB 9F ;Level 5
8020: DB A3 ;Level 6
8022: DB 9F ;Level 7
8024: DB A3 ;Level 8
8026: DB A7 ;Level 9

; ROM pointer for Underworld background and common sprites
8028: 1B 81 ; Pointer to Tiles_B_Dungeon (Underworld background)
802A: BB 9C ; Pointer to Tiles_S_Dungeon (Underworld common sprites)

; ROM pointer for Overworld background and sprites
802C: 3B 89 ; Pointer to Tiles_B_Surface (Overworld background)
802E: 5B 91 ; Pointer to Tiles_S_Surface (Overworld sprites)

; VRAM addresses
8030: 17 00 ; VRAM address for backgrounds
8032: 08 E0 ; VRAM address for shared "normal" creatures
8034: 09 E0 ; VRAM address for specific "normal" creatures
8036: 0C 00 ; VRAM address for bosses

; Byte counts for Overworld background and sprites (matches table 802C)
8038: 08 20 ; Size of backgrounds
803A: 07 20 ; Size of surface sprites

; Byte counts (matches table 8030)
803C: 08 20 ; Size of backgrounds
803E: 01 00 ; Size of shared "normal" creatures
8040: 02 20 ; Size of "normal" creatures
8042: 04 00 ; Size of bosses

Configure Tiles

ConfigureTiles: 
;
; Configure tiles based on the level from 0 (surface) to 9 (Ganon).
; Level is in $10.
;
; >$10=0  Tiles_B_Surface(820)->1700 and Tiles_S_Surface(720)->08E0 Set overworld background and sprites
;
;   ELSE  Tiles_B_Dungeon(820)->1700 and Tiles_S_Dungeon(100)->08E0   Underworld background and common sprites
;         8000[>$10]->8030[#2] (803C[#2])       Plus additional enemy sprites per level
;         8014[>$10]->8030[#3] (803C[#3])
;      Note that >$10 is non-0 here. First entry in tables 8000,8014 is never used.
;
8044: 20 25 E6        JSR     $E625               ; Turn off all video
8047: AD 02 20        LDA     P_STATUS            ; Clear address latch (and scroll)
804A: 20 5E 80        JSR     $805E               ; TLDR:Clear !SetCount
804D: A5 10           LDA     $10                 ; Get level number (0 is overworld)
804F: D0 13           BNE     +19 ($8064)         ; If dungeon, go to dungeon configure
;Surface configure
8051: 20 91 80        JSR     $8091               ; Set pointer & counter for overworld 1)background 2)sprites 
8054: 20 DC 80        JSR     $80DC               ; Copy tiles(skipping setting counter)
8057: AD 1D 05        LDA     !SetCount           ; \
805A: C9 02           CMP     #$02                ; !SetCount must be 2 to escape this loop
805C: D0 F3           BNE     -13 ($8051)         ; /
805E: A9 00           LDA     #$00                ; \ Sub called by 804A,Jump to here from 807D
8060: 8D 1D 05        STA     !SetCount           ; Clear !SetCount and leave
8063: 60              RTS                         ; /
;Dungeon configure
8064: 20 80 80        JSR     $8080               ; Set pointer for dungeon 1)background 2)sprites
8067: 20 CC 80        JSR     $80CC               ; Set counter and Copy tiles
806A: AD 1D 05        LDA     !SetCount           ; \
806D: C9 02           CMP     #$02                ; !SetCount must be 2 to escape this loop
806F: D0 F3           BNE     -13 ($8064)         ; /
8071: 20 AC 80        JSR     $80AC               ; TLDR:Creature sprites pointer into [$00:01]
8074: 20 CC 80        JSR     $80CC               ; Set counter and Copy tiles
8077: 20 BC 80        JSR     $80BC               ; TLDR:Boss sprites pointer into [$00:01]
807A: 20 CC 80        JSR     $80CC               ; Set counter and Copy tiles
807D: 4C 5E 80        JMP     $805E               ; TLDR:Clear !SetCount and leave

; Set ROM (source) pointer from table 8028. Table has 2 entries.
8080: AD 1D 05        LDA     !SetCount           ; \ Sub called by 8064(Dungeon configure)
8083: 0A              ASL     A                   ; Use !SetCount as pointer index
8084: AA              TAX                         ; /
8085: BD 28 80        LDA     $8028,X             ; \
8088: 85 00           STA     $00                 ; Get appropriate pointer
808A: E8              INX                         ; from table at $8028
808B: BD 28 80        LDA     $8028,X             ; put it at [$00:01]
808E: 85 01           STA     $01                 ; /
8090: 60              RTS                         ; Done

; Set ROM (source) pointer and length from tables 802C and 8038. Tables have 2 entries each.
8091: AD 1D 05        LDA     !SetCount           ; \ Sub called by 8051(Surface configure)
8094: 0A              ASL     A                   ; Use !SetCount as pointer index
8095: AA              TAX                         ; /
8096: BD 2C 80        LDA     $802C,X             ; \
8099: 85 00           STA     $00                 ;  \
809B: BD 38 80        LDA     $8038,X             ;   \
809E: 85 02           STA     $02                 ; Put appropriate pointers
80A0: E8              INX                         ; from tables at $802C and $8038
80A1: BD 2C 80        LDA     $802C,X             ; into [$00:01] and [$02:03] respectively
80A4: 85 01           STA     $01                 ;   /
80A6: BD 38 80        LDA     $8038,X             ;  /
80A9: 85 03           STA     $03                 ; /
80AB: 60              RTS                         ; Done

; Set ROM (source) pointer from 8000[>$10]. Table has 10 word entries.
80AC: A5 10           LDA     $10                 ; \ Sub called by 8071(Dungeon configure)
80AE: 0A              ASL     A                   ; Use level number as pointer index
80AF: AA              TAX                         ; /
80B0: BD 00 80        LDA     $8000,X             ; \
80B3: 85 00           STA     $00                 ; Get appropriate pointer
80B5: E8              INX                         ; from table at $8000
80B6: BD 00 80        LDA     $8000,X             ; put it at [$00:01]
80B9: 85 01           STA     $01                 ; /
80BB: 60              RTS                         ; Done

; Set ROM (source) pointer from 8014[>$10]. Table has 10 word entries.
80BC: A5 10           LDA     $10                 ; \ Sub called by 8077(Dungeon configure)
80BE: 0A              ASL     A                   ; Use level number as pointer index
80BF: AA              TAX                         ; /
80C0: BD 14 80        LDA     $8014,X             ; \
80C3: 85 00           STA     $00                 ; Get appropriate pointer
80C5: E8              INX                         ; from table at $8014
80C6: BD 14 80        LDA     $8014,X             ; put it at [$00:01]
80C9: 85 01           STA     $01                 ; /
80CB: 60              RTS                         ; Done

; Copy set of tiles to VRAM. 051D is the set pointer ... 0,1,2, or 3
; in table (803C is table of lengths, 8030 is table of VRAM address).
; Tile pattern ROM (source) pointer in 00:01.
; Immediately before each call to this sub, a sub is called...
; ...that writes a pointer to [$00:01]
80CC: AD 1D 05        LDA     !SetCount           ; \ Sub called by 8067,8074,807A
80CF: 0A              ASL     A                   ; Use !SetCount as pointer index
80D0: AA              TAX                         ; /
80D1: BD 3C 80        LDA     $803C,X             ; \
80D4: 85 02           STA     $02                 ; Get appropriate counter
80D6: E8              INX                         ; from table at $803C
80D7: BD 3C 80        LDA     $803C,X             ; put it at [$02:03]
80DA: 85 03           STA     $03                 ; /
;The above flows into the below, but the below might be called directly...
;...avoiding only the counter-creation at [$02:03] which was handled seperatly
80DC: AD 1D 05        LDA     !SetCount           ; \ Sub called by 8054
80DF: 0A              ASL     A                   ; Use !SetCount as pointer index
80E0: AA              TAX                         ; /
80E1: BD 30 80        LDA     $8030,X             ; \
80E4: 8D 06 20        STA     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; Get appropriate pointer
80E7: E8              INX                         ; from table at $8030
80E8: BD 30 80        LDA     $8030,X             ; put it in VRAM_ADDR
80EB: 8D 06 20        STA     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; /
80EE: A0 00           LDY     #$00                ; Empty out Y, it's never bumped...
;...but no non-register indirect lookup command exists, so this will have to do.
;Loop starts here. It copies stuff to VRAM_DATA. [$02:03] is a 16-bit countdown, not a pointer.
80F0: B1 00           LDA     (GP_00),Y           ; Branch to here from 8111,8115
80F2: 8D 07 20        STA     P_VRAM_DATA         ;
80F5: A5 00           LDA     $00                 ; \
80F7: 18              CLC                         ;  \
80F8: 69 01           ADC     #$01                ;   \
80FA: 85 00           STA     $00                 ; Bump+ pointer at [$00:01]
80FC: A5 01           LDA     $01                 ;   /
80FE: 69 00           ADC     #$00                ;  /
8100: 85 01           STA     $01                 ; /
8102: A5 03           LDA     $03                 ; \
8104: 38              SEC                         ;  \
8105: E9 01           SBC     #$01                ;   \
8107: 85 03           STA     $03                 ; Bump- counter at [$02:03]
8109: A5 02           LDA     $02                 ;   /
810B: E9 00           SBC     #$00                ;  /
810D: 85 02           STA     $02                 ; /
810F: A5 02           LDA     $02                 ; \
8111: D0 DD           BNE     -35 ($80F0)         ; If [$02:03] isn't empty...
8113: A5 03           LDA     $03                 ; ...go back to top
8115: D0 D9           BNE     -39 ($80F0)         ; /
8117: EE 1D 05        INC     !SetCount           ; Next set
811A: 60              RTS                         ; Done
;The entire rest of the bank is tile graphics
;Tiles_B_Dungeon:

A07E: 02 03 ;

A080: A6 14           LDX     $14                 ; \  (Looks like X should be even)
A082: BD 00 A0        LDA     MMC1_1,X            ;  Take Nth pointer from above...
A085: 85 00           STA     $00                 ;  ...copy it into [$00:01]
A087: BD 01 A0        LDA     MMC1_1+1,X          ;  N is the PPU Loading Index at $14
A08A: 85 01           STA     $01                 ; /
A08C: 20 F6 A0        JSR     MMC1_1+F6           ;
A08F: A9 3F           LDA     #$3F                ; A lot of writing to put...
A091: 8D 00 03        STA     $0300               ; ...3F 00 FF into [$0300:0302]...
A094: A2 00           LDX     #$00                ; ...and clearing the PPU Loading Index...
A096: 86 14           STX     $14                 ; ...and the Vertical Scrolling Offset?
A098: 86 5C           STX     <!FlipFlag          ; Don't let NMI flip name tables
A09A: 8E 01 03        STX     0301                ;
A09D: CA              DEX                         ; Underflow here?
A09E: 8E 02 03        STX     0302                ;
A0A1: 60              RTS                         ;

A0A2: 48              PHA                         ; Branch to here from A0FD
A0A3: 8D 06 20        STA     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; [NES] VRAM address select
A0A6: C8              INY                         ;
A0A7: B1 00           LDA     (GP_00),Y           ; 
A0A9: 8D 06 20        STA     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; [NES] VRAM address select
A0AC: C8              INY                         ;
A0AD: B1 00           LDA     (GP_00),Y           ; 
A0AF: 0A              ASL     A                   ;
A0B0: 48              PHA                         ; Have now added 2 bytes to the stack
A0B1: A5 FF           LDA     <CUR_2000           ; Current value of 2000 (PPUCTRL)
A0B3: 09 04           ORA     #$04                ; Increment VRAM by 32 (down)
A0B5: B0 02           BCS     MMC1_1+B9           ; Skip next line
A0B7: 29 FB           AND     #$FB                ; Increment VRAM by 1 (across)
A0B9: 8D 00 20        STA     P_CNTRL_1           ; Set new VRAM increment ...
A0BC: 85 FF           STA     <CUR_2000           ; ... and set cached value
A0BE: 68              PLA                         ; Pull Accumulator (added at A0B0)
A0BF: 0A              ASL     A                   ;
A0C0: 08              PHP                         ; Push Processor status!
A0C1: 90 03           BCC     MMC1_1+C6           ; If carry clear, skip 2 lines
A0C3: 09 02           ORA     #$02                ;
A0C5: C8              INY                         ;
A0C6: 28              PLP                         ; Pull Processor status (pushed at A0C0)
A0C7: 18              CLC                         ;
A0C8: D0 01           BNE     MMC1_1+CB           ; Skip next line
A0CA: 38              SEC                         ; Set carry flag
A0CB: 6A              ROR     A                   ;
A0CC: 4A              LSR     A                   ;
A0CD: AA              TAX                         ;
A0CE: B0 01           BCS     MMC1_1+D1           ; If carry set...
A0D0: C8              INY                         ; ...skip this Y-bumping
A0D1: B1 00           LDA     ($00),Y             ;
A0D3: 8D 07 20        STA     P_VRAM_DATA         ; [NES] VRAM data
A0D6: CA              DEX                         ;
A0D7: D0 F5           BNE     MMC1_1+CE           ;
A0D9: 68              PLA                         ; Pull accumulator (added at A0A2)
A0DA: C9 3F           CMP     #$3F                ;
A0DC: D0 0C           BNE     MMC1_1+EA           ; Skip next 4 lines
A0DE: 8D 06 20        STA     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; [NES] VRAM address select
A0E1: 8E 06 20        STX     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; [NES] VRAM address select
A0E4: 8E 06 20        STX     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; [NES] VRAM address select
A0E7: 8E 06 20        STX     P_VRAM_ADDR         ; [NES] VRAM address select
A0EA: 38              SEC                         ; \  Branch to here from A0DC
A0EB: 98              TYA                         ;  \
A0EC: 65 00           ADC     $00                 ;   \
A0EE: 85 00           STA     $00                 ; Adding Y+1 to pointer at [$00:01]
A0F0: A9 00           LDA     #$00                ;   /
A0F2: 65 01           ADC     $01                 ;  /
A0F4: 85 01           STA     $01                 ; /

A0F6: AE 02 20        LDX     P_STATUS            ; [NES] PPU status, JSR to here from A08C
A0F9: A0 00           LDY     #$00                ;
A0FB: B1 00           LDA     (GP_00),Y           ; Why this opcode when Y is known to be 0?
A0FD: 10 A3           BPL     MMC1_1+A2           ; Branch up to A0A2
